Technical Training Class

OCT 5 8:00 AM – 9:00 AM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Level Up Your Customer Service: Webex Calling Customer Assist

Technical Training Class: Foundation

This session empowers attendees to understand and implement a basic voice-only contact center using Webex Calling Customer Assist. We'll explore the advantages over standard voice queues, demonstrating how it enhances the caller experience, agent efficiency, and overall operational effectiveness. Through core concepts, a practical walkthrough, benefit analysis, and diverse use cases, participants will learn to build and leverage a simple voice hub for improved customer service and professionalism. The session concludes with a recap and actionable next steps for exploring Webex Calling Customer Assist.

CE credits: 1

Speakers

Cisco

Susan Parrish

Webex Enablement Specialist, Cisco

Susan Parrish is a Technical Enablement Specialist at Cisco with over 20 years of experience in technology and customer-facing roles. Specializing in Webex solutions, she bridges the gap between complex software and practical application through effective adult education strategies. A 5-star instructor at WebexOne since its inception in 2022 and a 2025 Visionary Award nominee, Susan is also an active member of Women of Cisco and Women in Collaboration. She holds a Master’s in Adult Education from East Carolina University and a BS in Management of Information Systems from Mount Olive University. Outside of her professional work, she enjoys tailgating at ECU football games, traveling, and spending time at the beach.

Technical Training Lab

OCT 5 8:00 AM – 12:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Making Zero Downtime a Reality with Webex Calling

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

Unlock enterprise-grade reliability in Webex Calling with Local and Survivability Gateways. The Local Gateway provides flexible, registration-based cloud-to-premises connectivity, while the Survivability Gateway acts as a critical failover mechanism during outages. By leveraging their co-residency, organizations can build resilient voice environments that maintain continuous service. This session examines the architecture, integration, and proactive management of these features, providing the technical foundation for high-availability hybrid voice deployments.

CE credits: 4

Technical Training Lab

OCT 5 8:00 AM – 12:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Deep-Dive into Cisco RoomOS xAPIs (Option 1)

Technical Training Lab: Foundation

This hands-on lab focuses on teaching different integration points—SSH, HTTP, WebSockets, Cloud xAPI, Macros—for RoomOS endpoint customization and automation. You’ll learn xAPI fundamentals, UI extensions, and web content. Plus, you’ll build your own customization using Macros and begin your deployment journey, introducing Control Hub's ability to deploy customizations and using third-party tooling that leverages the same xAPI to deploy at scale.

CE credits: 3

Speakers

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1753881752153001CmL5/attprofile/TimK_Headshots-1_1783694937064001Hy6J.jpeg)

Cisco

Tim Kratzke

Technical Leader, Cisco

Tim Kratzke has been working on Cisco Telepresence products for over 14 years, ranging from Immersive video systems, video infrastructure such as Cisco Meeting Server (CMS) and Expressway, and for the past 8 years specializing in video endpoints. After working as a Telepresence TAC engineer, Tim now works with global CX teams training new engineers, building tools and automation to aid in the troubleshooting process, and working with engineering to improve the serviceability of collaboration video endpoints. In his spare time Tim is an avid hockey fan and enjoys traveling and exploring new destinations.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1753822718448001S5fi/attprofile/Edit_1786738106527001FDbL.png)

Cisco

Bobby McGonigle Jr

Technical Marketing Engineer, Cisco

I’m a Technical Marketing Engineer in Cisco’s Devices Business Unit, focusing on RoomOS products designed for integrators, including the Codec Pro G2 and Codec EQ. My interest in collaboration technology began during a university internship, where my first exposure to professional audiovisual systems centered on Cisco. I was hooked.

I’m now in my fifth year at Cisco, where I specialize in RoomOS xAPIs, macros, and room design. Before joining Cisco, I spent four years supporting RoomOS devices in education, an environment where complex technology needs to feel simple and natural for teachers. That experience still shapes my work today. I like to think of myself as a Cisco customer with a little extra access, helping make a product I already love even better.

One project I’m especially proud of is the Join Zoom macro. I originally created it at the university to make joining Zoom SIP meetings easier for my users, then shared it with the wider Cisco community. After joining Cisco, I had the opportunity to watch that idea evolve into an official product capability. The final implementation was not my code, but it solved the same problem and reflected the user experience I had set out to create. The macro has since been retired in favor of the current Join Zoom solution, but I’m proud that something I built for my users served the wider community and lived a full life.

That journey captures what I enjoy most about RoomOS. I want to help people discover what they can create with it. My goal as a speaker is to take people with little or no coding or xAPI experience and give them the confidence to interact with RoomOS devices like professionals.

Technical Training Lab

OCT 5 8:00 AM – 9:30 AM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Contact Center Enterprise (CCE) Observability with Splunk and ThousandEyes: From Business KPIs to Incident Triage

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

This hands-on session explores how Splunk can serve as a single observability destination for on-premises Contact Center Enterprise (CCE), consolidating operational reporting data, application logs, and network telemetry into one platform. Attendees will learn how to create business-level KPIs using CCE real-time and historical data, and then correlate these metrics with application and infrastructure signals. This approach enables the development of dashboards for executive reporting and engineering troubleshooting, helping to accelerate incident triage with a unified evidence base.

CE credits: 3

Gururaj B T

Technical Leader, Cisco

Gururaj B T is a Technical Leader in Cisco's Contact Center Business Unit, with over 17 years of experience in architecting and delivering enterprise contact center solutions. Technical Training Class

OCT 5 8:00 AM – 9:00 AM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Deploy at Scale: Automating Secure Provisioning for Collaboration Endpoints

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Scaling cloud-registered collaboration endpoints requires balancing deployment speed with enterprise-grade security. In this class, you'll explore how automated certificate enrollment using Simple Certificate Enrollment Protocol (SCEP) and Cisco ISE, combined with the Zero Touch Network Onboarding (ZTNO) app, creates an effortless, zero-touch onboarding experience for Cisco video devices. Learn how organizations can deploy devices at scale while enforcing enterprise security, identity, and trust through certificate-based 802.1X authentication.

CE credits: 2

Technical Training Class

OCT 5 9:15 AM – 10:15 AM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Deploying Secure AI for UCCE and Private Cloud Environments

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Organizations running on-premises UCCE and private cloud environments face a critical challenge: how to adopt generative AI without compromising security, data sovereignty, or control. This session provides a technical and strategic framework for deploying AI agents in high-security contact center environments while preserving the integrity of existing infrastructure.

We will explore four key areas. First, you'll learn how Customer Voice Portal (CVP) and existing call flows integrate with Cisco AI Studio, including the technical handoffs and orchestration between legacy logic and modern AI services.

Next, you'll learn how to address data sovereignty and security by defining clear boundaries between private cloud environments and externally processed workloads to help close the trust gap.

We'll then explore strategic AI orchestration and how to move beyond a single-bot model toward a multi-agent architecture. Topics include Silent Transfer, Announced Transfer and MCP handoffs, as well as how to establish boundaries for agent specialization.

Finally, we'll cover governance and advanced observability, including how to implement Cisco's AI Impact Assessment framework to manage fairness and safety while proactively monitoring for hallucinations, accuracy drift, and system prompt changes.

You'll leave with a clearer path for modernizing UCCE with secure, observable AI while maintaining control over infrastructure, data, and deployment-and a practical framework for bridging legacy reliability with autonomous customer experience.

CE credits: 2

Unifying the Webex Platform with Webex Contact Center and Webex Calling

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

This technical session explores the architecture and capabilities of a unified Webex platform. We will examine how Webex Contact Center and Webex Calling streamline infrastructure, simplify deployments, and power seamless customer experiences. We will begin with a brief overview of today’s Webex Contact Center Common Edge Services, followed by a quick look at future use cases that Webex Common Media Services will enable.

The core of the session will focus on the technical mechanics of Webex Contact Center Common Edge Services. We will dive deep into PSTN and routing options including Cloud Connected PSTN (CCP) and Bring Your Own PSTN (BYOPSTN). We'll cover the Edge Connect architecture to bring media to Webex using private link, as well as global deployment strategies with a spotlight on supporting operations in India.

Finally, we will cover key cross-platform integrations designed to optimize operations. You will learn about advanced reporting that captures Webex Calling call legs, Webex Calling device control, WebRTC enhancements, and Webex Messaging for agent-to-supervisor collaboration. We will conclude with roadmap highlights.

CE credits: 2

Cisco Devices: The Backbone of the Intelligent Workplace

Technical Training Class: Foundation

Did you know you might be spending thousands on workplace sensors and automation tools that are already available in Cisco collaboration devices? Powered by industry-leading NVIDIA chips, Cisco devices go beyond superior audio and video to serve as the backbone of the modern, intelligent workplace.

But an intelligent workplace is about more than the technology in the room—it’s about how we design spaces around the way people work today. Take a virtual walk through Cisco’s PENN 1 office and other Cisco workplaces to explore real-world spaces, the technology behind them, and the design decisions that bring them to life. Along the way, we’ll look at how Cisco devices enable smarter workspace management, IoT, automation, sustainability, and better employee experiences. We'll also explore where the intelligent workplace is headed next.

Learn how to create an office that becomes a “magnet, not a mandate” by bringing together people, spaces, and technology—often using capabilities you already own.

Calling Interoperability with Microsoft Teams

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Many organizations rely on both Microsoft Teams and Cisco calling platforms. This session shows you how to get the best of both.

Learn how Cisco Call for Microsoft Teams enables seamless interoperability between Microsoft Teams and Cisco calling platforms. We'll cover the client-side integration in depth, explore common customer deployment scenarios, and compare alternative approaches, including Teams Phone with Direct Routing and SIP Gateway for Cisco Phones.

Whether you're planning a deployment or optimizing an existing environment, you'll leave with a practical understanding of the available integration options and when to use each.

Topics include:

\- Cisco Call for Microsoft Teams

\- Teams Phone with Direct Routing and SIP Gateway for Cisco Phones

\- Common customer deployment scenarios and best practices

A basic understanding of Cisco calling platforms and the Webex App is recommended.

CE credits: 2

Automating Workflows with AI Agents in Webex Suite

Technical Training Class: Foundation

Attendees will learn how Webex Suite AI Agents reduce manual effort and automate workflows across the collaboration lifecycle. Examples include scheduling meetings through natural language and creating Jira tickets from action items. The session will explain what each agent does, the problem it solves, and how agents support scheduling, note capture, in-meeting decisions, post-meeting automation, and live system integrations.

Attendees will then explore how the Media Intelligence Real-Time Media Streaming API provides direct access to live Webex meeting audio streams. Topics covered include the Media Intelligence platform architecture, authentication flow, and audio streaming.

Together, these capabilities show how Webex can support automated collaboration workflows and new AI experiences using live meeting media.

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Beyond the AI Pilot: A Flight Plan for Trusted CX at Scale

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

AI pilots are multiplying but trusted, measurable value at scale remains elusive. The challenge is not choosing another AI capability; it is creating an operating model that connects technology, people, data, governance, and outcomes across the customer journey.

In this intermediate session, contact center architects, administrators, and CX leaders will use the F.L.I.G.H.T. framework to move beyond experimentation: Find the right use case, Launch deliberately, Interact and tune, Govern and secure, Handoff with context, and Transform through measurable outcomes. Grounded in Webex Contact Center and informed by real deployments, end-to-end walkthroughs reveal the decisions that separate pilots from durable operations. This includes: where AI belongs, how it should be tuned and measured, when humans should enter the journey, and how trust is maintained as autonomy grows. Leave with practical principles for scaling AI without losing context, control, or sight of business value.

CE credits: 2

Tim Kratzke

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1753822718448001S5fi/attprofile/Edit_1786738106527001FDbL.png)

Cisco

Bobby McGonigle Jr

Technical Marketing Engineer, Cisco

I’m a Technical Marketing Engineer in Cisco’s Devices Business Unit, focusing on RoomOS products designed for integrators, including the Codec Pro G2 and Codec EQ. My interest in collaboration technology began during a university internship, where my first exposure to professional audiovisual systems centered on Cisco. I was hooked.

I’m now in my fifth year at Cisco, where I specialize in RoomOS xAPIs, macros, and room design. Before joining Cisco, I spent four years supporting RoomOS devices in education, an environment where complex technology needs to feel simple and natural for teachers. That experience still shapes my work today. I like to think of myself as a Cisco customer with a little extra access, helping make a product I already love even better.

One project I’m especially proud of is the Join Zoom macro. I originally created it at the university to make joining Zoom SIP meetings easier for my users, then shared it with the wider Cisco community. After joining Cisco, I had the opportunity to watch that idea evolve into an official product capability. The final implementation was not my code, but it solved the same problem and reflected the user experience I had set out to create. The macro has since been retired in favor of the current Join Zoom solution, but I’m proud that something I built for my users served the wider community and lived a full life.

That journey captures what I enjoy most about RoomOS. I want to help people discover what they can create with it. My goal as a speaker is to take people with little or no coding or xAPI experience and give them the confidence to interact with RoomOS devices like professionals.

AI Agents and Securing the Agentic Future

Technical Training Class: Foundation

Agentic AI is transforming enterprise systems by enabling AI to plan, reason, and act across tools and workflows. In this session, we'll demystify agentic architectures, from augmented Large Language Models (LLMs) to multi-agent collaboration using protocols like the Model Context Protocol (MCP). We'll then address the critical challenge: security. As agents gain autonomy, risks like prompt injection and tool misuse increase. You'll learn practical design patterns and a simple framework for building secure, scalable agentic AI systems in real-world environments.

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

In this hands-on lab, attendees will build a real Webex Contact Center flow from scratch using provided lab computers and sandbox environments. Starting with Flow Designer fundamentals, the lab progresses through connecting to an external API, integrating a Webex AI Agent, and adding an external Model Context Protocol (MCP) server connection. The finale puts it all together: using the new Flow Designer MCP server to build and modify flows through natural language with AI tools like Claude or ChatGPT. No prior Flow Designer experience required. Leave with a working flow and the skills to build your own.

CE credits: 3

AI for On-Prem for Contact Center Enterprise: Cisco Native and BYO AI with Universal Harness

Technical Training Class: Expert

In this session, we’ll introduce Cisco’s on-premises AI solution for Contact Center Enterprise (CCE). You’ll see how to build reusable AI capabilities with Universal Harness, package them as portable services, and deploy them wherever customers need them—across the Cisco cloud, Cloud Connect/Fusion, Cisco AI POD, or bring-your-own (BYO) AI infrastructure.

You’ll learn how Universal Harness supports both Cisco-native AI services and customer-selected speech-to-speech or BYO AI providers, including OpenAI Realtime, Gemini Live, Nova Sonic, Azure Voice Live, and other pluggable models. You’ll leave with a reference architecture, provider integration patterns, and a practical blueprint for delivering real-time voice AI in CCE while preserving customer control, deployment flexibility, and a path to a fully on-premises AI deployment with Cisco AI POD.

Troubleshooting Cisco Webex Calling

Technical Training Class: Expert

This session dives into troubleshooting Webex Calling, from end-user onboarding, synchronization, and management to location, number, and dial plan configuration. We'll cover Public Switched Telephone Network (PSTN) connectivity including premises-based PSTN, focusing on Webex Calling Multi-Tenant (Dedicated Instance not included). Learn to troubleshoot using Control Hub, Multiplatform Phones (MPP), the Webex App, Cisco Devices, and SIP trunks. We'll tackle media connectivity and quality diagnostics, walk through the tools available to pinpoint issues fast, and explore APIs to automate provisioning and troubleshooting.

CE credits: 3

Agile Management of Webex Calling: There's an API for That

Technical Training Class: Expert

Webex Calling offers flexible management options, ranging from individual configuration in Control Hub to bulk provisioning and powerful public APIs. This session covers the technical essentials, including Representational State Transfer (REST), JavaScript Object Notation (JSON), serialization and deserialization, integrations, scopes, and Open Authorization (OAuth) flows. You'll learn best practices for building custom provisioning tools using a Python Software Development Kit (SDK) designed to simplify Webex Calling API consumption. Finally, we'll explore how AI can accelerate the development of your custom solutions, empowering you to manage your collaboration environment with greater speed and agility.

Technical Training Class

OCT 6 3:30 PM – 5:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Workforce Management for the Infinite Workforce

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

AI agents introduce a theoretically unlimited workforce into the contact center, which breaks every assumption traditional workforce management (WFM) was built on. In this 90-minute technical exploration, see how Webex WFM is purpose-built for the hybrid era: ML-powered forecasting that factors AI deflection rates alongside human volume, AI-optimized scheduling that matches agents to complexity rather than raw AHT, and a real-time intraday control plane that manages human and AI agents from a single surface. We'll demonstrate predictive SLA alerts with "what-if" simulations, one-click staffing actions, and automated surplus reallocation showing how workforce planning evolves from reactive queue-watching to proactive, intent-driven orchestration.

CE credits: 2

Quick Takes

OCT 6 5:00 PM – 5:20 PM CT

Are you investing in AI or Business Outcomes?

Walk away with specific actions to close the gap between AI hype and AI ROI

Think about the last time you were on hold and got bounced between three systems before a human even picked up. That's not a customer experience problem. That's an integration problem, and most AI vendors are making it worse, not better.

Cisco and Verint have spent decades building the opposite: one workforce, working in one solution, with agentic AI embedded where the work actually happens instead of layered on top of it.

Join us for a candid look at what the gap between AI hype and AI ROI, and what it actually takes to close it.

Quick Takes

OCT 6 5:25 PM – 5:45 PM CT

Instructing the Agent: The Knowledge Layer Behind Trusted CX AI

Cisco has put real AI capabilities in the hands of CX leaders today, but what that capability has exposed is a problem sitting one layer down: the knowledge these systems run on is fragmented, inconsistent, and ungoverned.

An AI agent is only as reliable as what it has been given. Outdated policies and procedures, duplicate content, and conflicting instructions produce wrong answers to customers, failed automation, and compliance risk, eroding trust in the brand.

This session looks at how CX leaders build a trusted instruction layer for enterprise AI. Drawing on hyper-scale deployments, eGain will show how to move past traditional knowledge management to AI-ready infrastructure, so Cisco customers get accurate answers and actions every time.

Quick Takes

OCT 6 5:50 PM – 6:10 PM CT

C.A.R.E.S.: A New Standard for Measuring Human + AI Contact Center Performance

One independent framework for measuring performance across all agents

Is a contained AI interaction really a successful interaction? And are humans always better than AI? As AI agents handle more customer conversations, contact centers need a common standard for measuring performance across the human + AI workforce.

This session introduces the C.A.R.E.S. framework: Compliance, Accuracy, Resolution, Effort and Sentiment. Learn how to compare human and AI performance by customer intent, determine where AI outperforms humans—and where it doesn’t—and identify opportunities to improve both.

See how SuccessKPI AI AutoQM and interaction analytics apply these measures at scale, uncover failures traditional metrics miss, and provide independent performance reporting and governance.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1754669205851001a3Iy/attprofile/Jeetu-Patel_1785434244942001FWHY.jpg)

Cisco

Jeetu Patel

President & Chief Product Officer, Cisco

Jeetu Patel is Cisco’s President and Chief Product Officer. He combines a bold vision, steeped in product design and development expertise, operational rigor, and innate market understanding to create high growth businesses. He is relentlessly focused on building world class products that solve Cisco customers’ biggest problems—bringing the power of the Cisco portfolio together to connect and protect every aspect of their organization in the era of AI. Previously he was Cisco’s Executive Vice President and General Manager of Security and Collaboration where he led the strategy and development for these businesses and held P&L responsibility for the multibillion-dollar portfolio. In this role, Jeetu led with his creative vision and intense focus on innovation and swift execution.

Together with his team, he transformed and positioned our Security and Collaboration portfolios for success and growth. In both areas, he reinvigorated organic innovation, championed key inorganic investments, and drove simplification across these portfolios with a fanatical focus on design and user experience. Prior to joining Cisco in 2020, Jeetu was the Chief Product Officer (CPO) and Chief Strategy Officer (CSO) at Box, a role he pioneered. He led the company’s product and platform strategy, setting the company’s long-term vision and roadmap for cloud content management in the enterprise. He transformed Box from a single product application to a multi-product platform used by 100K customers representing 69% of the Fortune 500. The discipline, quality standards, performance metrics, and stability Jeetu instilled fueled the platform’s growth – nearly quadrupling revenues to $700M+. Box’s growth scaled to reach over 60M users with over 50% of customers using multiple products.

He also created the Box Platform business unit where he led product strategy, marketing and developer relations – driving products from incubation stage to mature offerings. Before joining Box, Jeetu was General Manager and Chief Executive of EMC’s newly acquired Syncplicity business unit, a cloud service for Enterprise File Sync Sharing (EFSS) and collaboration. One of the first SaaS-based solutions offered by EMC, Jeetu spearheaded the company’s acquisition. He created a world class leadership team, secured some of the market’s largest customers and led the group to become one of the fastest growing EFSS companies in a highly competitive market. Other key roles at EMC included CMO for the Information Intelligence Group and Chief Strategy Officer, where he drove the organic and inorganic strategy for the division’s cloud and mobile growth. Previously, Jeetu was President of Doculabs, a research and advisory firm co-owned by Forrester Research. The firm focused on collaboration and content management across a range of industries including financial services, insurance, energy, manufacturing, and life sciences. He currently serves on the board of JLL, an American commercial real estate services company. Jeetu holds a B.S. in Information Decision Sciences from the University of Illinois, Chicago, and lives in the San Francisco Bay Area with his family.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1754587189411001Hmwr/attprofile/Snorre-Kjesbu_1785434231275001oWd1.jpg)

Cisco

Snorre Kjesbu

SVP & GM, Collaboration, Cisco

Snorre Kjesbu is Senior Vice President & General Manager of Collaboration Devices at Cisco. He is a global citizen leading a worldwide organization responsible for the collaboration devices business ranging from IP phones to immersive video systems. Snorre’s passion for design with over 20 years of engineering experience are at the core of his belief in always providing authentic user experiences. Prior to his return to Cisco, Snorre was Executive VP of Design, Creation and Fulfillment at BANG & OLUFSEN in Copenhagen. His résumé also includes SVP at Tandberg and being responsible for R&D on wireless communication at ABB.

He and his team at ABB were awarded the Wall Street Journal Innovation award for their work on wireless sensors in 2002. Snorre holds a Master of Science from the University of Bristol and has been a guest lecturer at the Stanford Network Research Center in Stanford University. He has obtained more than 20 patents in the areas of communications and video conferencing and is on the board of directors for several IT companies. When not working, Snorre can likely be seen on a pair of skis – whether on snow or water.

Arvind Jain

Technical Training Lab

OCT 7 10:30 AM – 12:30 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

AI-Powered Meetings: Build a Follow-Up Agent and Troubleshooting Assistant with Webex Model Context Protocol (MCP)

Technical Training Lab: Foundation

Imagine an AI agent that turns your meeting transcript into a follow-up Webex space with action items, or takes a meeting number and hands you back a quality analysis in seconds. Webex's new Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers make this possible by giving AI agents direct access to meeting data, transcripts, and messaging. In this hands-on lab, you'll learn what MCP is, enable the servers on a Webex org, and build two workflows: one that auto-creates a follow-up space from a transcript, and one that analyzes meeting quality and generates an HTML troubleshooting report.

CE credits: 2

Quick Takes

OCT 7 11:00 AM – 11:20 AM CT

Context is King: The Next Generation of Customer-Centric AI Agents

How to create highly personalized, seamless CX experiences.

This session takes a deeper look into how to leverage AI Concierge to create highly personalized, seamless experiences that move beyond basic self-service and embrace sophisticated agentic systems.

The most effective AI agents combine three critical capabilities: persistent memory, contextual awareness, and multi-agent orchestration. Together, these capabilities transform AI from a transactional tool into an intelligent digital workforce capable of understanding nuanced customer needs, adapting to changing circumstances, and delivering precise, effective resolutions. By designing AI agents that can remember, reason, coordinate, and act, organizations can create differentiated customer experiences that drive higher satisfaction, faster problem resolution, and stronger long-term loyalty.

The future of customer engagement lies not in single AI models, but in orchestrated ecosystems of intelligent agents working together to deliver truly personalized outcomes at scale.

Amey leads Product Management for Cisco Calling including cloud Calling, on-premise Unified Communications Manager (UCM) and Broadworks. Cisco calling is an easy to use, mobile integrated, enterprise grade, innovative calling phone system that is easy to setup and manage for IT admins, with best-in-class security & global availability.

He is passionate about innovation and evolving the calling experience from hearing, to seeing, to doing; empowering people to have a richer calling experience with Webex and leveraging cloud capabilities for the new era of hybrid work.

Prior to Cisco, Amey built Microsoft Teams calling and meetings from ground up and grew Teams adoption to millions of users. He also led Product management for Microsoft's Azure cloud IaaS/PaaS services across different verticals segments including sovereign clouds and government verticals. He has extensive experience working at large companies as well as startups, building innovative products and bringing them to market.

Amey brings more than 25 years of experience in areas such as product management, design, cloud engineering in productivity software at Microsoft Office, natural language processing, machine learning with search and NUI experiences, hardware integration for audio and video and analytics. He has developed 20 patents over his career.

Amey has a B.Tech from Indian Institute of Technology, Bombay and a Masters from North Carolina State University. He lives in Seattle with his family and enjoys outdoor activities such as hiking and kayaking in his free time.

Quick Takes

OCT 7 12:30 PM – 12:50 PM CT

Beyond Agents: Building the Relationship Enterprise

The operating systems for managing customer relationships at scale.

Enterprises have created many agents, yet customer relationships remain fragmented. Conversations lose context, interactions restart, and no one owns the relationship end-to-end. A new operating system can connect existing systems, workflows, departments, and agents—so context and continuity travel with the customer. Agents run on it. Departmental systems become tools it can invoke. The result: white-glove experiences at enterprise scale, growing more personal with every interaction. The future of AI is one relationship that remembers, understands, and endures.

Future-Proofed Workplaces: Powering an AI-Ready Infrastructure

How to Get Your AI Agent to Do What You Actually Want: A Prompt Engineering Session

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

LLM-based AI agents are powerful but can be hard to control: They may ask for information users don’t have or fail to follow procedures correctly, even when those procedures are clearly defined in the instructions or knowledge base. This session shows how to design prompts that guide agents to behave reliably and do what you actually want.

CE credits: 2

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Bridging the Gap: Seamless Coexistence of On-Premises and Cloud Collaboration Solutions

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

As organizations modernize their collaboration environments, the path from on-premises solutions (like Cisco UCM, Unity Connection, and Microsoft Active Directory) to cloud-based services (such as Webex Calling and Entra ID) introduces new challenges and opportunities. This session explores architectural patterns and best practices for hybrid deployments that deliver a seamless, secure, and productive user experience. Attendees will learn how to maintain coexistence, enable interoperability, and plan for a phased transition to the cloud.

CE credits: 2

Webex Edge Connect: Mastering Design and Deployment for Meetings, Calling, and Dedicated Instance

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Webex Edge Connect provides private network peering that bypasses the public Internet, directly linking your network to the Webex backbone for Meetings and Calling services, enabling your organization to gain access to the latest Webex innovations.

In this session, we'll cover the Edge Connect solution in detail, including key design considerations for different architecture types, deployment best practices, and the latest updates to the offering.

A foundational understanding of Webex Meetings and Calling products is recommended to fully benefit from the session content.

Modernize Your On-Prem Contact Center

A practical path to AI, digital channels, and the cloud.

Learn how to layer Webex AI Agent, AI Assistant, and digital-first channels directly onto your existing setup to drive immediate operational impact, boost agent productivity, and deliver the personalized support your customers expect today.

OCT 7 1:30 PM – 2:00 PM CT

The AI Concierge Advantage: Delivering Personalized CX at Scale

Delivering great CX through hyper-personalized AI agents.

AI is reshaping customer experience—from the contact center to every customer touchpoint across the enterprise. As AI assistants, AI agents, and intelligent workforce solutions become embedded throughout the customer journey, organizations have a unique opportunity to deliver faster service and more personalized experiences for stronger business outcomes.

As AI takes on a more active role in customer interactions, the opportunity is to move beyond individual agents toward an AI Concierge focused on managing customer experiences at scale. We’ll explore how AI memory and intelligent orchestration can connect context across people, agents, and enterprise systems to create more personal, continuous experiences—while maintaining the trust, security, governance, and human oversight needed to scale with confidence. We’ll also hear from Community Health in a fireside chat about how they’re putting AI agents into practice and building trusted AI experiences for their customers.

Speakers

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1753855589691001lSX5/attprofile/Professional%20pic%20-%20new_1786371360779001gPrd.jpg)

Cisco

Manoj Muthavarapu

Director, Product Management, Cisco

Manoj leads the product team shaping AI agents for customer experience at Cisco, with a focus on building intelligent, autonomous experiences that transform how businesses serve their customers. A full-stack developer turned product manager, he joined Cisco through the IMImobile acquisition and is based in Hyderabad, India, where he lives with his wife and son. Outside work, he enjoys cricket, travel, and good conversations over coffee.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1755886161417001XhKi/attprofile/Messenger_CHNw_Bio_Pic%281%29_1785945543414001C6id.jpg)

Community Health Network

Tim Messenger

Team Manager, Unified Communications - Contact Center, Community Health Network

Tim Messenger has spent two decades supporting the telephony environment at Community Health Network. Prior to working in IT, Tim spent time doing low voltage cabling and phone system installation, as well as a few years working in Emergency Medicine before returning to the world of IT. Initially starting as a contractor at CHNw for the E911 location mapping project, then joining the Voice Services/UC Team as an analyst supporting Nortel, and eventually Cisco Call Manager and UCCX. In 2012, he accepted a position as a Contact Center analyst supporting the UCCE IP/IVR environment. Then assisted in the migration to UCCE CVP.

In 2017, a specific Contact Center Team was created, with Tim as the manager. During this time, he and his team assisted in many environment upgrades, support, and enhancements. In 2024, CHNw began the process of building the first WxCC contact centers, setting the stage for a full migration from UCCE. Tim and his team migrated over 120 contact centers and over 400 IVRs as well as deploying centralized scheduling and triage for Primary Care practices. During this they also worked with Cisco and Presidio to develop the first deployment of WxCC AI for a pilot program for a rescheduling agent integrated with the existing practice IVRs. After the pilot was completed, the UC Contact Center Team deployed the AI Rescheduling Agent to an additional 50 practices.

Currently, they are working on the next use cases covering New Appointment Scheduling, Registration, as well as Bill Pay/Payment Plans. Tim and his team continue to evolve to support the growing changes of the Contact Center world to help ensure the patients and care givers at Community Health Network have the best experience and care as possible.

Hands-On AI in Webex Contact Center: Building Connected Experiences from Self-Service to Agent Assistance

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

Modern customer experience is moving from reactive bots to proactive, agentic systems. This hands-on lab walks through eight technical missions: Topic Analytics for friction discovery, autonomous AI Agents in Webex AI Studio, agent-to-agent collaboration via MCP, the Cisco AI Assistant for human augmentation, AI routing for outcome-driven agent matching, and AI Quality Management evaluating 100% of interactions. Built for IT leaders and CX architects ready to stop managing isolated AI tools and start building a connected experience. Leave with working configurations and operational patterns.

Roundtable

OCT 7 1:50 PM – 3:05 PM CT

AI Across Industries: What's Working, What's Stalled, and What's Next

How AI is showing up in real workflows, team experiences, and customer-facing moments, and what leaders are learning along the way

The most interesting AI conversations are happening in the messy middle: where early ideas meet real teams, real workflows, and real customer expectations. This roundtable brings leaders together to trade concrete use cases across industries — where an approach proven in financial services might transfer to government, or a solution built for healthcare could reshape retail. The value is practical cross-pollination: surfacing applications one industry has already solved that others haven't yet tried.

Speakers

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1777646173880001fcKG/attprofile/mrankin_1784214462906001ZWxX.jpg)

Cisco

Mark Rankin

Distinguished Systems Engineer, Cisco

Mark Rankin is a Distinguished Systems Engineer and a senior technical leader in the Cisco Global Collaboration Sales Centre of Excellence. His current focus is on Cisco's overall collaboration strategy, and he is currently focused on the impact of AI, Mobility, and aspects relating to SaaS data residency, sovereignty, and air-gap environments. Mark has a particular interest in calling and real-time communications and how this has, and continues to, evolve. Mark was the lead architect during the development of the incubation project that became Cisco Hosted Collaboration Solution (HCS). Prior to his current role, Mark was the lead architect for Cisco's private 5G incubation project.

Over his career, Mark has worked with some of the world's largest service providers to help them transform their networks and services from TDM to IP and now towards Cloud, NFV, SDN, and Automated platforms. Mark has expertise in Fixed and Mobile Networks, WAN, LAN, DC/Cloud, SP Voice, Collaboration, and IoT services. Mark has 25 years of experience in the service provider marketplace. He holds a BSc in Mathematical Physics from UMIST and an MSc in Computer Science from the University of Manchester.

VP, Voice Services, First Horizon Bank

Jason O'Dell currently serves as VP, Voice Services for First Horizon Corporation. O’Dell is driven to discover innovative, cost-effective and risk-compliant solutions that improve the company’s ability to serve clients and communities.

Abdalnafie Sibaie

AI Specialist, Team Lead, CarShield

Sibaie is the AI Specialist and Team Lead of the AI Team at CarShield, where he leads the design and deployment of AI across the contact center and beyond. His team builds production voice AI agents, AI-powered quality assurance pipelines, agentic applications, and workflow automations spanning Cisco Webex Contact Center, Azure AI Foundry, and modern voice AI platforms.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1756138638608001Kegd/attprofile/JoeMcQueen%20Headshot_1786390947935001phWp.JPG)

American Eagle

Joe McQueen

Sr Manager of Collaboration, American Eagle

Joseph McQueen leads enterprise collaboration strategy at American Eagle Outfitters, where he has driven full Webex Suite adoption across a complex retail environment of 1,000+ stores and 4,200 users. With more than 15 years of continuous Webex experience dating back to Cisco’s 2007 acquisition, he has overseen the transition from CUCM to Webex Calling, the standardization of 300+ custom conference rooms, and the integration of Meetings, Messaging, Webinars, and Contact Center into daily retail operations.

Joseph is the architect behind AEO’s extensive Webex automation platform. His team has built 50+ custom bots and API integrations that have transformed store provisioning from a one-hour manual process into a five-minute, fully consistent workflow. These solutions connect Webex with Meraki, ServiceChannel, Jira, and internal store systems to deliver automated device provisioning, intelligent ticket enrichment, real-time alerting, and AI-assisted support. The same platform is now expanding into Webex Contact Center AI agents designed to reduce seasonal staffing needs and preserve institutional knowledge.

His work emphasizes practical scale: 4,572 devices under management, “Real Vision Rooms” combining Cisco Codec EQ, Pro Ceiling Microphones, and wireless cameras, and the integration of Cisco EQ into AEO’s Town Hall for accessible large-scale Webex Webinars. The results include dramatically faster deployments, higher operational consistency, and a measurable shift of support effort from repetitive tasks to higher-value problem solving.

At Webex One, Joseph shares a retail practitioner’s view of what full-suite Webex adoption looks like when automation, device strategy, and AI are treated as core operational capabilities rather than side projects.

Technical Training Class

OCT 7 3:30 PM – 4:30 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Under the Hood: How Cisco Deployed Webex Contact Center and AI Agents for Intelligent Automation

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Cisco TAC set out to bring the same intelligent automation powering web and chat to the voice channel. Using Webex Contact Center's built-in AI Agents, we replaced traditional IVR menus with natural language intent detection and built autonomous case creation flows complete with real-time entitlement checks and optional SMS-based input. This session covers our journey from migration through iterative proof-of-concept (POC) refinement and the design decisions that worked—and those that didn't. For the leadership perspective on shifting from infrastructure pathway to intelligent solution, see our companion session: From Infrastructure to Intelligence: How Cisco Reimagined Voice Support with AI Agents.

CE credits: 2

Enhancing Patient Experience and ROI with Webex Contact Center and AI

An Interactive Healthcare Jeopardy Challenge with Prizes

Healthcare organizations must improve patient access and satisfaction while managing staffing shortages, rising interaction volumes, and operational complexity.

Through practical healthcare scenarios, attendees will explore intelligent self-service, virtual agents, real-time agent assistance, automated summaries, digital engagement, and advanced routing. These capabilities will be tied to measurable outcomes, including lower call volume, shorter handle times, improved first-contact resolution, increased productivity, and higher patient satisfaction.

Carry Your Own Future: How Enterprises Are Winning with BYOC on Webex

What the shift to Bring Your Own Carrier really means for enterprise Webex deployments — and the channel partners driving it.

You're on Webex — or you're evaluating it. And the question of who owns your carrier relationship is one of the most consequential decisions in that deployment.

54% of enterprises have already adopted Bring Your Own Carrier. In this session, Bandwidth and three of Webex's top channel partners share what they've seen from customers who made that move: what triggered it, what the migration looked like, and what changed on the other side in cost, control, and flexibility.

No slides. No product demo. Just partners who've been in the room when enterprise customers made this call.

Bring AgenticOps to Life with Control Hub and Cisco Devices

Automate management, troubleshoot faster, and simplify operations with AI.

IT teams are stretched thin by complex troubleshooting, growing ticket queues, and the daily work of managing devices, workspaces, users, and collaboration services.

In this session, you’ll learn how new AI capabilities in Control Hub help teams automate common tasks, catch issues earlier, and move from symptoms to resolution faster. You'll also learn how you can continually optimize your workspaces to increase user adoption.

We’ll cover how Cisco Cloud Control brings Control Hub into a unified AgenticOps platform, giving admins easier access to a simplified experience. You’ll also learn about new innovations like AI Canvas for natural language troubleshooting, and Control Hub AI integration with agent platforms including Microsoft Copilot and Amazon Quick.

You’ll walk away from this session with clear examples of use cases your teams can try now.

Nirav Chanchani

Director, Product Management, Cisco

Nirav is a Director of Product Management at Cisco, and manages a team responsible for Control Hub - the management platform for the Webex Collaboration suite of products. This encompasses the areas of partner and customer onboarding, user management, administration, licensing, and service provisioning across all the Webex GTM channels.

Nirav joined Cisco as a product manager helping build and then successfully launching Cisco’s cloud-based instant messaging product, Webex Messenger/Jabber. OCT 7 4:45 PM – 5:05 PM CT

The Deepfake Crisis In Real-time Communication

How to help defend against AI fraud and improve customer experience

Is your organization prepared to defend against AI attacks? With bots, attackers are now empowered to impersonate executives or genuine customers, infiltrate hiring processes, manipulate helpdesks, and exploit the trust placed in familiar faces and voices. Over 74% of organizations have been hit by a deepfake, and most didn’t know it at the time.

Join Pindrop’s SVP of Global Partner Sales Sumant Mauskar to learn how deepfake attacks are trending, why traditional security is no longer effective, and how leading organizations are working to better secure their interactions across every real-time communication channel: from the contact center, to internal phone systems, to virtual meeting rooms.

OCT 8 7:45 AM – 8:30 AM CT

Women in Technology Panel: The Next Era of Leadership

Join women leaders for a candid, forward-looking conversation about the next era of leadership.

All attendees will receive priority seating at the keynote! Grab breakfast and join a future-focused conversation with inspiring women about leadership in the age of AI.

What do we keep, what do we leave behind, and what do we build next?

The world around us is changing. Technology is reshaping how work gets done. Expectations of work, careers, and leaders are evolving. Traditional hierarchies are giving way to new ways of collaborating and influencing. And qualities once considered “soft,” from empathy and curiosity to judgment and trust, are becoming increasingly essential.

All of it raises a bigger question: Does our definition of great leadership need to change, too?

For decades, conversations about women and leadership have often focused on how women can succeed within existing systems. But as those systems evolve, we have an opportunity to ask something different: What if we helped shape what comes next?

Join women leaders from technology, business, and the nonprofit sector for a candid, forward-looking conversation about the next era of leadership. We'll explore what great leadership will require in a world transformed by AI and other forces of change; which traditional expectations of leaders are worth carrying forward and which may be holding us back; and how we can build a model of leadership that is more human, sustainable, and inclusive by design.

The future of leadership isn't predetermined. We have an opportunity to shape it now.

Technical Training Lab

OCT 8 8:00 AM – 12:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Securing Webex with Identity Management, Zero Trust Encryption, and Data Compliance

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

Learn how to configure and manage Webex identity and security capabilities, such as:

Identity, Authentication, and Authorization: Provision Webex users from Cisco Duo using SCIM 2.0, explore settings and licensing templates, and configure SSO using both SAML and OIDC

End-to-End (E2E) Encryption: Examine Webex zero trust end-to-end encrypted meetings and calling, along with media watermarking and deepfake detection

Compliance: Investigate compliance for Webex Meetings, Messaging, and Calling, including data retention, eDiscovery with Webex Compliance Hub, and DLP/AMP with Secure Access

CE credits: 4

OCT 8 8:00 AM – 12:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Webex Calling Administration Professional exam \[Partner Only\]

The Webex Calling Administration Professional exam for Cisco Partners validates that you can deploy, configure, and maintain Webex Calling environments in production. It is designed for IT professionals, system administrators, and partner engineers responsible for day-to-day operations.

The exam tests hands-on competency across the core administrative domains: user and device management, call flow and dial plan configuration, feature provisioning, and troubleshooting. Passing confirms you can stand up a working deployment, optimize it for performance, and resolve issues without escalation. For Partners, the credential is a concrete signal of delivery capability. It demonstrates that your team can implement Webex Calling correctly the first time, reduce support overhead, and support customer growth on cloud telephony. Credentialed administrators bring verified expertise in Webex Calling architecture, configuration, and best practices.

Troubleshooting Webex Video Integration for Microsoft Teams

VIMT works great — until it doesn't. When devices end up stuck in the Teams lobby as "unverified," the root cause is rarely the device itself. TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Webex as an Agentic Platform: MCP, A2A, and the Future of Intelligent Automation

Technical Training Class: Foundation

AI agents are only as useful as the tools and workflows they can reach.

In this session, we'll explore Webex as an agentic platform. You'll learn how external tools connect into Webex via inbound Model Context Protocol (MCP), how Webex exposes its own capabilities to AI agents via outbound MCP, and what's on the horizon with Agent-to-Agent (A2A) workflows. You'll see live demos of MCP in action and walk away knowing how to build it for your organization. Whether you're a Webex power user, administrator, or developer, this session provides the foundation to plan for the agentic future.

CE credits: 1

Quick Takes

OCT 8 11:25 AM – 11:45 AM CT

Inside Cisco Spaces at WWT: The Architecture Behind a Smarter Workplace

Webex devices, network telemetry, and the platform tying it together

Cisco Spaces turns Wi-Fi and Meraki telemetry, Webex device data, and occupancy sensors into a live picture of how a building actually gets used — but only if the architecture underneath is built right.

This session opens the hood: the location layer (Wi-Fi/Meraki telemetry), the sensor layer (Webex Rooms, Navigator, people-count and environmental data), and the integration layer (the Spaces connector and APIs feeding booking and workplace systems).

We'll cover the design decisions that separate a deployment that just collects data from one that drives wayfinding, rooms ready before people arrive, and hybrid meetings where remote colleagues are equal participants — including what WWT learned running this exact stack at scale.

Modernize Your Contact Center: Route Business Processes and Bring Your Own Channel in Webex Contact Center

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Organizations across all verticals want to improve customer service by adding new channels. The Webex Contact Center Work Items Framework makes this possible by routing any type of interaction giving organizations the flexibility to adapt and deliver on their business outcomes.

At the heart of this framework is Bring Your Own Channel (BYOC), which lets organizations extend Webex Contact Center to any digital messaging platform, like Webex Messaging, Microsoft Teams, or LINE.

We'll explore real-world use cases and live demos showing how to onboard new channels and turn every interaction into a measurable business outcome.

Technical Training Class

OCT 8 12:45 PM – 1:45 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

Considerations For Designing Rooms with AVoIP

Technical Training Class: Intermediate

In this session, we’ll explore Cisco’s IP peripheral portfolio including how each device works and how IP peripherals differ from traditional directly connected room peripherals. We’ll also look at the operational advantages including simplified support, centralized monitoring, lifecycle management, and deeper visibility through Control Hub.

The session will move from products to architecture, showing how Cisco collaboration and Cisco networking come together to make advanced room experiences possible. With Cisco switches providing the IP foundation for power, connectivity, segmentation, QoS, discovery, and reliable transport, collaboration devices can be placed flexibly, managed consistently, and scaled across many room types. We’ll highlight how this combined Cisco architecture enables cleaner deployments, better troubleshooting, and more predictable experiences.

We’ll also explore practical design patterns for advanced spaces, including distributed audio and video, divisible rooms, and rooms that can operate independently or combine into larger collaboration environments using the same IP infrastructure. We’ll show how Cisco speakers, microphones, and cameras work together to enable richer audio experiences, smarter camera behavior, and more natural meetings.

Attendees will leave with a clear understanding of how AVoIP works, how Cisco IP peripherals and Cisco networking products complement each other, and what to consider when designing reliable, manageable, and flexible collaboration rooms at scale.

CE credits: 2

Technical Training Lab

OCT 8 1:00 PM – 2:00 PM CT

TRAINING PASS REQUIRED

From AI Agent to AI Workforce: Scaling Beyond the First Bot

Technical Training Lab: Intermediate

You've built your first AI agent. Now what? In this session, we'll explore how to evolve from a single virtual assistant into a fully scaled AI workforce within your contact center. Learn how to design multiple specialized AI agents, enable handoffs between bots and human agents, and create end-to-end customer journeys. We'll cover real-world architecture patterns and best practices for scaling AI. Whether you're expanding an existing deployment or planning your next phase, this session will give you the practical insights needed to move from isolated automation to enterprise-wide impact.

CE credits: 2

Speakers

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1752685105157001AB63/attprofile/Headshot26_1784302021371001k4QN.jpg)

Cisco

Krishna Tyagi

Senior Technical Leader, Webex CX & AI FDE, Cisco

Krishna Tyagi is a Senior Technical Leader within the Webex Customer Experience AI FDE at Cisco Systems, working across the full Webex CX portfolio — including Webex Contact Center, AI Agent, AI Assistant, and AI Quality Management. He guides enterprise organisations through the complete AI lifecycle, from architecture and design through large-scale deployment and optimisation, partnering closely with Cisco's Product, Engineering, and AI Strategy teams.

With 21 years of industry experience, Krishna has advised C-suite leaders at organisations through complex, large-scale AI transformations. He is a Distinguished Cisco Live Speaker and a recipient of the Cisco-wide Innovation Award, presented by Cisco's CEO. He holds Cisco Generative AI Blue Belt and Business AI Practitioner credentials, is an IEEE Senior Member, and regularly publishes thought leadership on AI and customer experience. Krishna holds a Bachelor's degree in Electronics and Telecommunications Engineering and an Executive Leadership certification from Cornell University.

Breakout

OCT 8 1:00 PM – 1:30 PM CT

Better Service Starts with Simpler Operations: Lessons from Financial Services CX Leaders

How leading institutions are improving customer and employee experiences

What can every industry learn from financial services? In highly competitive markets where customer trust matters and service expectations continue to rise, financial services organizations are often among the first to rethink how customer service is delivered.

In this rapid-fire discussion, customer leaders share practical lessons from improving customer and employee experiences, simplifying operations, and preparing for what's next. Attendees will leave with real-world ideas that can be applied across any contact center environment.

Closing Keynote: Webex Customer Awards

Join us for the 5th Annual Webex Customer Awards ceremony

This isn't just an awards show; it’s a front-row seat to the future of work. Witness how the world’s most forward-thinking organizations are using Webex and Cisco technology to push boundaries, reimagine collaboration, and drive real-world impact.

Speakers

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1754587189411001Hmwr/attprofile/Snorre-Kjesbu_1785434231275001oWd1.jpg)

Cisco

Snorre Kjesbu

SVP & GM, Collaboration, Cisco

Snorre Kjesbu is Senior Vice President & General Manager of Collaboration Devices at Cisco. He is a global citizen leading a worldwide organization responsible for the collaboration devices business ranging from IP phones to immersive video systems. Snorre’s passion for design with over 20 years of engineering experience are at the core of his belief in always providing authentic user experiences. Prior to his return to Cisco, Snorre was Executive VP of Design, Creation and Fulfillment at BANG & OLUFSEN in Copenhagen. His résumé also includes SVP at Tandberg and being responsible for R&D on wireless communication at ABB.

He and his team at ABB were awarded the Wall Street Journal Innovation award for their work on wireless sensors in 2002. Snorre holds a Master of Science from the University of Bristol and has been a guest lecturer at the Stanford Network Research Center in Stanford University. He has obtained more than 20 patents in the areas of communications and video conferencing and is on the board of directors for several IT companies. When not working, Snorre can likely be seen on a pair of skis – whether on snow or water.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1754587189080001HTJK/attprofile/brian-stout-cisco_1784300731306001Lh6H.jpeg)

Cisco

Brian Stout

SVP, Collaboration and Workplace Experience, Cisco

Brian Stout leads Cisco’s Global Collaboration Sales and Go-to-Market organization and is responsible for the Collaboration product sales specialists, inside sales, pre-sales engineering, channel and ecosystem strategy, and routes to market. Cisco Collaboration’s $6B business is a critical part of the company’s overall SaaS transformation plan and is one of Cisco’s most competitive market segments.

Brian and his team are helping to redefine Cisco’s SaaS business strategy to bring subscription-based solutions to customers and partners. During his Cisco career, Brian has held several strategic sales roles, starting as an Enterprise Account Manager, working with key customers in the financial industry. As the Director of Operations in the Americas, leading the Commercial Segment, he helped redefine the go-to-market strategy for the US commercial business to drive consistent, strong revenue growth.

Brian led the Americas Collaboration Sales team, delivering technology transformation and hybrid work innovation while accelerating growth in the region before becoming Vice President of the Global Collaboration Sales organization. He is an inspirational leader known for building high-performing teams and laser-focused on driving results.

Closing Keynote: Luminary Fireside Chat with Tom Brady

Tom Brady takes the stage

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1782146280666001WJCS/attprofile/TB_HeadShot%20%282%29_1782146783580001qxkz.png)

Tom Brady

7x Super Bowl Champion, Entrepreneur & Philanthropist

Tom Brady is one of the most accomplished athletes of all time, celebrated for his unmatched focus, discipline, and sustained excellence. A seven-time Super Bowl champion, he holds the record for the most titles by a quarterback and has made 10 Super Bowl appearances and 18 division titles, earning him a place among the greatest competitors in sports history. Brady has since built a legacy as an entrepreneur, broadcaster, author, and philanthropist, currently serving as lead analyst for FOX Sports' NFL broadcast.

![](https://static.rainfocus.com/webex/webexone2026/att/1754669123647001UZxN/attprofile/Anurag-Dhingra_1785434340683001ub3D.jpg)

Cisco

Anurag Dhingra

SVP & GM, Networking & Collaboration, Cisco

During his tenure with Cisco, Anurag has held several leadership roles in the Collaboration business, including Chief Technology and Product Officer where he led the entire Collaboration software business. He was also Vice President and Head of Engineering for Webex Meetings where he was responsible for delivering applications and services to power online meetings for millions of users worldwide.

With over two decades of experience in the Communications, Collaboration, and SaaS industries, Anurag is accomplished at building high performance global teams and creating innovative, market leading products. Anurag takes pride in being a customer-focused leader, with a proven track record of strategic thinking and executing with a strong culture of innovation.

Passionate about applying technology for good, Anurag serves on Cisco's Human Rights Advisory Council and was the founding executive sponsor of Cisco's Responsible AI Framework.

He holds a bachelor’s degree in Electronics & Communications Engineering from National Institute of Technology, Kurukshetra, India.
